No, not really. I mean, my wife has tried, of course. Like I was saying a minute ago, she tells me the late meetings and those little changes in her work messages are just normal office stuff. And I try to take her word for it, I really do, but... well, for the past year, they’ve just lined up too precisely to feel accidental.

So, I guess I'm here to talk about that.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been noticing these patterns in your wife's behavior?\n\nPatient: Well, like I mentioned a moment ago, it's been about a year now. It really started right around the time she went back to working outside the home again.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe your wife is actually sending you these hidden signals, or could there be another explanation for the patterns you're seeing?\n\nPatient: I've tried to think of other explanations, I really have. I mean, I know she tells me it's just normal office stuff, like I said earlier. But... yes, I do believe she's actually sending them. \n\nWhen you sit down and look at the late meetings and those specific little changes in her messages... they just line up way too precisely to be an accident. It really feels like a private signal meant just for me, like she's trying to tell me something she just won't say directly to my face. So, no, I haven't really found another explanation that makes sense of it all.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period where you didn't believe your wife was sending you these hidden signals?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. Um... once I started noticing it about a year ago, it's just been pretty consistent. I haven't really had a block of time where it went away or where I thought I was wrong about it. It's just... it's been there ever since she went back to the office.\n\nDoctor: Has anything or anyone ever been able to convince you, even for a short time, that your wife is not sending you these hidden signals?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, my wife has tried, of course. Like I was saying a minute ago, she tells me the late meetings and those little changes in her work messages are just normal office stuff. And I try to take her word for it, I really do, but... well, for the past year, they’ve just lined up too precisely to feel accidental. \n\nEven if I try to brush it off for a day or someone tells me I'm overthinking it, I just can't ignore the pattern. It always comes back to feeling like there’s a private signal in those messages, meant to tell me something she just won’t say directly. So, no, no one has been able to talk me out of it.\n\nDoctor: Do you have any other thoughts or beliefs that you think might be true, even if others disagree with you?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. Um... it's really just this situation with my wife. Everything else in my life is pretty standard. I go to work, help my students, come home. I don't have any other... well, theories or anything like that. It's strictly just what we've been talking about today. Nothing else comes to mind.\n\nDoctor: Has this belief about your wife sending you hidden signals affected your daily life or relationships in any significant way?\n\nPatient: Well, it's definitely affected our marriage. We've had a lot of tense conversations about it at home. It gets pretty stressful because we just keep going in circles. Actually, it got to the point where we started seeing a couples therapist, and she's the one who eventually suggested I come in for an individual assessment. \n\nBut outside of that... no, not really. Work is fine. I'm still doing my job as a teaching assistant without any issues, and I'm keeping up with my usual routines, taking care of myself. It's really just the strain it's putting on things between me and my wife.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals ever changed in intensity or certainty over the past year?\n\nPatient: Not really. It's been pretty consistent. I mean, maybe there are days where I'm busier at work and I don't think about it quite as much in the moment, but the certainty hasn't really changed. Since I first started noticing the pattern about a year ago, it's just been a steady fact. I haven't really had any periods where I doubted it or felt more strongly about it—it's just always there, exactly the same.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like someone else, besides your wife, is hiding something from you or sending you secret messages?\n\nPatient: No, not at all. Like I said, it's really just this one specific thing with my wife. I don't think anyone else is doing anything like that. My coworkers, my students, friends... everything is completely normal with them. No secret messages or anything hidden. It's just her.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Never bundle several symptoms, or several attributes of one symptom, into a single question — e.g. asking whether a symptom is present and how long it has lasted at the same time.\n- If you need to ask about more than one thing, ask the one that tells you the most now and come back to the rest later.\n- Keep the question clear enough for the patient to understand.\n- Respond in English.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"intent\": \"<what you want to find out with this question>\", \"question\": \"<how you actually ask it in the interview>\"}\n"

To me, it really seems like there’s a private signal in them, like they're meant to tell me something she just won’t say directly. So, I guess I'm here to talk about that.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been noticing these patterns in your wife's behavior?\n\nPatient: Well, like I mentioned a moment ago, it's been about a year now. I mean, I know she tells me it's just normal office stuff, like I said earlier. But... yes, I do believe she's actually sending them. \n\nWhen you sit down and look at the late meetings and those specific little changes in her messages... they just line up way too precisely to be an accident. It really feels like a private signal meant just for me, like she's trying to tell me something she just won't say directly to my face. So, I guess I'm here to talk about that.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been noticing these patterns in your wife's behavior?\n\nPatient: Well, like I mentioned a moment ago, it's been about a year now. It really started right around the time she went back to working outside the home again.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe your wife is actually sending you these hidden signals, or could there be another explanation for the patterns you're seeing?\n\nPatient: I've tried to think of other explanations, I really have. I mean, I know she tells me it's just normal office stuff, like I said earlier. But... yes, I do believe she's actually sending them. \n\nWhen you sit down and look at the late meetings and those specific little changes in her messages... they just line up way too precisely to be an accident. It really feels like a private signal meant just for me, like she's trying to tell me something she just won't say directly to my face. So, no, I haven't really found another explanation that makes sense of it all.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period where you didn't believe your wife was sending you these hidden signals?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. Um... once I started noticing it about a year ago, it's just been pretty consistent. I haven't really had a block of time where it went away or where I thought I was wrong about it. It's just... it's been there ever since she went back to the office.\n\nDoctor: Has anything or anyone ever been able to convince you, even for a short time, that your wife is not sending you these hidden signals?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, my wife has tried, of course. Like I was saying a minute ago, she tells me the late meetings and those little changes in her work messages are just normal office stuff. And I try to take her word for it, I really do, but... well, for the past year, they’ve just lined up too precisely to feel accidental. \n\nEven if I try to brush it off for a day or someone tells me I'm overthinking it, I just can't ignore the pattern. It always comes back to feeling like there’s a private signal in those messages, meant to tell me something she just won’t say directly. So, no, no one has been able to talk me out of it.\n\nDoctor: Do you have any other thoughts or beliefs that you think might be true, even if others disagree with you?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. Um... it's really just this situation with my wife. Everything else in my life is pretty standard. I go to work, help my students, come home. I don't have any other... well, theories or anything like that. It's strictly just what we've been talking about today. Nothing else comes to mind.\n\nDoctor: Has this belief about your wife sending you hidden signals affected your daily life or relationships in any significant way?\n\nPatient: Well, it's definitely affected our marriage. We've had a lot of tense conversations about it at home. It gets pretty stressful because we just keep going in circles. Actually, it got to the point where we started seeing a couples therapist, and she's the one who eventually suggested I come in for an individual assessment. \n\nBut outside of that... no, not really. Work is fine. I'm still doing my job as a teaching assistant without any issues, and I'm keeping up with my usual routines, taking care of myself. It's really just the strain it's putting on things between me and my wife.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals ever changed in intensity or certainty over the past year?\n\nPatient: Not really. It's been pretty consistent. I mean, maybe there are days where I'm busier at work and I don't think about it quite as much in the moment, but the certainty hasn't really changed. Since I first started noticing the pattern about a year ago, it's just been a steady fact. I haven't really had any periods where I doubted it or felt more strongly about it—it's just always there, exactly the same.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like someone else, besides your wife, is hiding something from you or sending you secret messages?\n\nPatient: No, not at all. Like I said, it's really just this one specific thing with my wife. I don't think anyone else is doing anything like that. My coworkers, my students, friends... everything is completely normal with them. No secret messages or anything hidden. It's just her.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other thoughts that you were completely certain of, but others around you did not believe?\n\nPatient: No, I can't say that I have. It's really just this one situation with my wife. I don't have any other... unusual beliefs or anything like that. Everything else in my life is just normal, everyday stuff. So, no, nothing else that people have disagreed with me on like this.\n\nDoctor: Has there ever been any evidence that contradicted your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals?\n\nPatient: Well, she denies it, of course. She says the late meetings and those little changes in how she words her work messages are just normal office stuff. But... I mean, for the past year, they've just lined up too precisely. It doesn't feel accidental at all. It's like there's this private signal in them, meant to tell me something she just won't say directly to my face. So, sure, she says it's nothing, but the pattern is right there.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals could be wrong?\n\nPatient: I mean... no, I really don't think I'm wrong. I know she says it's just normal office stuff, the late meetings and the way she words things. But like I said, for the past year, it's just too precise. It doesn't feel accidental at all. It's like there's a private signal in those messages, trying to tell me something she won't just say to my face. I just can't see how I could be wrong when the pattern is so clear.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. If it did not change, leave \"note\" empty.\n- If the candidates have narrowed to one and you are confident enough that no further questioning is needed, return true for is_final.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"candidates\": [\"<ICD-10 code>\", \"...\"], \"note\": \"<why the list changed, or empty>\", \"is_final\": <true or false>}\n"
  },

And I try to take her word for it, I really do, but... well, for the past year, they’ve just lined up too precisely to feel accidental. \n\nEven if I try to brush it off for a day or someone tells me I'm overthinking it, I just can't ignore the pattern. It always comes back to feeling like there’s a private signal in those messages, meant to tell me something she just won’t say directly. So, no, no one has been able to talk me out of it.\n\nDoctor: Do you have any other thoughts or beliefs that you think might be true, even if others disagree with you?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. Um... it's really just this situation with my wife. Everything else in my life is pretty standard. I go to work, help my students, come home. I don't have any other... well, theories or anything like that. It's strictly just what we've been talking about today. Nothing else comes to mind.\n\nDoctor: Has this belief about your wife sending you hidden signals affected your daily life or relationships in any significant way?\n\nPatient: Well, it's definitely affected our marriage. We've had a lot of tense conversations about it at home. It gets pretty stressful because we just keep going in circles. Actually, it got to the point where we started seeing a couples therapist, and she's the one who eventually suggested I come in for an individual assessment. \n\nBut outside of that... no, not really. Work is fine. I'm still doing my job as a teaching assistant without any issues, and I'm keeping up with my usual routines, taking care of myself. It's really just the strain it's putting on things between me and my wife.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals ever changed in intensity or certainty over the past year?\n\nPatient: Not really. It's been pretty consistent. I mean, maybe there are days where I'm busier at work and I don't think about it quite as much in the moment, but the certainty hasn't really changed. Since I first started noticing the pattern about a year ago, it's just been a steady fact. I haven't really had any periods where I doubted it or felt more strongly about it—it's just always there, exactly the same.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like someone else, besides your wife, is hiding something from you or sending you secret messages?\n\nPatient: No, not at all. Like I said, it's really just this one specific thing with my wife. I don't think anyone else is doing anything like that. My coworkers, my students, friends... everything is completely normal with them. No secret messages or anything hidden. It's just her.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other thoughts that you were completely certain of, but others around you did not believe?\n\nPatient: No, I can't say that I have. It's really just this one situation with my wife. I don't have any other... unusual beliefs or anything like that. Everything else in my life is just normal, everyday stuff. So, no, nothing else that people have disagreed with me on like this.\n\nDoctor: Has there ever been any evidence that contradicted your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals?\n\nPatient: Well, she denies it, of course. She says the late meetings and those little changes in how she words her work messages are just normal office stuff. But... I mean, for the past year, they've just lined up too precisely. It doesn't feel accidental at all. It's like there's this private signal in them, meant to tell me something she just won't say directly to my face. So, sure, she says it's nothing, but the pattern is right there.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals could be wrong?\n\nPatient: I mean... no, I really don't think I'm wrong. I know she says it's just normal office stuff, the late meetings and the way she words things. But like I said, for the past year, it's just too precise. It doesn't feel accidental at all. It's like there's a private signal in those messages, trying to tell me something she won't just say to my face. I just can't see how I could be wrong when the pattern is so clear.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals ever changed, even slightly, since you first started noticing the patterns?\n\nPatient: No, it really hasn't changed. If anything, it's just stayed exactly the same. I know she keeps telling me the late meetings and the way she words her messages are just normal office stuff, but I've been seeing it for a year now. They just line up too precisely. It doesn't feel accidental at all. It's a private signal, meant to tell me something she just won't say directly. So, no, my belief hasn't wavered, because the pattern hasn't changed.\n\nDoctor: Have you considered the possibility that your interpretation of your wife's actions is not based in reality?\n\nPatient: I... no, I haven't really considered that it's not real. I mean, I know how it sounds, and I know she says the late meetings and the way she words her emails are just regular office stuff. But... I've been watching this for a year. It's just too precise. It doesn't feel like an accident or a coincidence. It feels real to me. It's like there's a private signal in there, trying to tell me something she won't just come out and say. So, no, I don't think I'm disconnected from reality. I just think I'm seeing something that's actually there.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. My coworkers, my students, friends... everything is completely normal with them. No secret messages or anything hidden. It's just her.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other thoughts that you were completely certain of, but others around you did not believe?\n\nPatient: No, I can't say that I have. It's really just this one situation with my wife. I don't have any other... unusual beliefs or anything like that. Everything else in my life is just normal, everyday stuff. So, no, nothing else that people have disagreed with me on like this.\n\nDoctor: Has there ever been any evidence that contradicted your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals?\n\nPatient: Well, she denies it, of course. She says the late meetings and those little changes in how she words her work messages are just normal office stuff. But... I mean, for the past year, they've just lined up too precisely. It doesn't feel accidental at all. It's like there's this private signal in them, meant to tell me something she just won't say directly to my face. So, sure, she says it's nothing, but the pattern is right there.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals could be wrong?\n\nPatient: I mean... no, I really don't think I'm wrong. I know she says it's just normal office stuff, the late meetings and the way she words things. But like I said, for the past year, it's just too precise. It doesn't feel accidental at all. It's like there's a private signal in those messages, trying to tell me something she won't just say to my face. I just can't see how I could be wrong when the pattern is so clear.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals ever changed, even slightly, since you first started noticing the patterns?\n\nPatient: No, it really hasn't changed. If anything, it's just stayed exactly the same. I know she keeps telling me the late meetings and the way she words her messages are just normal office stuff, but I've been seeing it for a year now. They just line up too precisely. It doesn't feel accidental at all. It's a private signal, meant to tell me something she just won't say directly. So, no, my belief hasn't wavered, because the pattern hasn't changed.\n\nDoctor: Have you considered the possibility that your interpretation of your wife's actions is not based in reality?\n\nPatient: I... no, I haven't really considered that it's not real. I mean, I know how it sounds, and I know she says the late meetings and the way she words her emails are just regular office stuff. But... I've been watching this for a year. It's just too precise. It doesn't feel like an accident or a coincidence. It feels real to me. It's like there's a private signal in there, trying to tell me something she won't just come out and say. So, no, I don't think I'm disconnected from reality. I just think I'm seeing something that's actually there.\n\nDoctor: Do you think other people would see the same hidden signals in your wife's behavior if they looked at it closely?\n\nPatient: I don't know if other people would catch it, honestly. Like I've been saying, she tells me the late meetings and those little changes in her work messages are just normal office stuff. And to an outsider, it probably just looks exactly like that. \n\nBut... I've been seeing this for a year now, and the way they line up is just too precise to be accidental. It's a private signal. It's meant to tell me something she won't say directly. So, someone else might miss it completely, because the message is specifically directed at me, not them.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. It doesn't feel accidental at all. It's like there's this private signal in them, meant to tell me something she just won't say directly to my face. So, sure, she says it's nothing, but the pattern is right there.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals could be wrong?\n\nPatient: I mean... no, I really don't think I'm wrong. I know she says it's just normal office stuff, the late meetings and the way she words things. But like I said, for the past year, it's just too precise. It doesn't feel accidental at all. It's like there's a private signal in those messages, trying to tell me something she won't just say to my face. I just can't see how I could be wrong when the pattern is so clear.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals ever changed, even slightly, since you first started noticing the patterns?\n\nPatient: No, it really hasn't changed. If anything, it's just stayed exactly the same. I know she keeps telling me the late meetings and the way she words her messages are just normal office stuff, but I've been seeing it for a year now. They just line up too precisely. It doesn't feel accidental at all. It's a private signal, meant to tell me something she just won't say directly. So, no, my belief hasn't wavered, because the pattern hasn't changed.\n\nDoctor: Have you considered the possibility that your interpretation of your wife's actions is not based in reality?\n\nPatient: I... no, I haven't really considered that it's not real. I mean, I know how it sounds, and I know she says the late meetings and the way she words her emails are just regular office stuff. But... I've been watching this for a year. It's just too precise. It doesn't feel like an accident or a coincidence. It feels real to me. It's like there's a private signal in there, trying to tell me something she won't just come out and say. So, no, I don't think I'm disconnected from reality. I just think I'm seeing something that's actually there.\n\nDoctor: Do you think other people would see the same hidden signals in your wife's behavior if they looked at it closely?\n\nPatient: I don't know if other people would catch it, honestly. Like I've been saying, she tells me the late meetings and those little changes in her work messages are just normal office stuff. And to an outsider, it probably just looks exactly like that. \n\nBut... I've been seeing this for a year now, and the way they line up is just too precise to be accidental. It's a private signal. It's meant to tell me something she won't say directly. So, someone else might miss it completely, because the message is specifically directed at me, not them.\n\nDoctor: Has there ever been a time when you felt your belief about your wife sending hidden signals was fading away?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I feel like I'm repeating myself a bit here, but it hasn't faded at all. I know my wife says the late meetings and the little changes in her work messages are just normal office stuff. I hear her when she tells me that. But... for the past year, they’ve just lined up way too precisely to feel accidental. It still feels exactly like there’s a private signal in them, meant to tell me something she won’t say directly. I mean, she already tries to do that. Like I've mentioned a few times now, she constantly tells me that the late meetings and the little changes in her work messages are just normal office stuff. That is her explanation.

Actually, it got to the point where we started seeing a couples therapist, and she's the one who eventually suggested I come in for an individual assessment. \n\nBut outside of that... no, not really. Work is fine. I'm still doing my job as a teaching assistant without any issues, and I'm keeping up with my usual routines, taking care of myself. It's really just the strain it's putting on things between me and my wife.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals ever changed in intensity or certainty over the past year?\n\nPatient: Not really. It's been pretty consistent. I mean, maybe there are days where I'm busier at work and I don't think about it quite as much in the moment, but the certainty hasn't really changed. Since I first started noticing the pattern about a year ago, it's just been a steady fact. I haven't really had any periods where I doubted it or felt more strongly about it—it's just always there, exactly the same.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like someone else, besides your wife, is hiding something from you or sending you secret messages?\n\nPatient: No, not at all. Like I said, it's really just this one specific thing with my wife. I don't think anyone else is doing anything like that. My coworkers, my students, friends... everything is completely normal with them. No secret messages or anything hidden. It's just her.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other thoughts that you were completely certain of, but others around you did not believe?\n\nPatient: No, I can't say that I have. It's really just this one situation with my wife. I don't have any other... unusual beliefs or anything like that. Everything else in my life is just normal, everyday stuff. So, no, nothing else that people have disagreed with me on like this.\n\nDoctor: Has there ever been any evidence that contradicted your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals?\n\nPatient: Well, she denies it, of course. It doesn't feel accidental at all. It's like there's this private signal in them, meant to tell me something she just won't say directly to my face. So, sure, she says it's nothing, but the pattern is right there.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about your wife sending you hidden signals could be wrong?\n\nPatient: I mean... no, I really don't think I'm wrong. It's like there's a private signal in those messages, trying to tell me something she won't just say to my face.
